 #include <cstring>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
 
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
-inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
+__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j,
+                                                  const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
 }
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                          \
+    do {                                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (call);                                                    \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                          \
+            fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,                  \
+                    cudaGetErrorString(cudaStatus));                                              \
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                              \
+        }                                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Matrix entries are stored as matrix[column][row].  Keeping threadIdx.x on the
+// row dimension makes every warp's global loads and stores contiguous.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tileDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int tilePath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t i = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t j = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool inBounds = i < numNodes && j < numNodes;
+
+    if (inBounds) {
+        tileDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+        tilePath[localIndex] = path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        tileDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        tilePath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = tileDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       tileDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = tileDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && inBounds && candidate < current) {
+            tileDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            tilePath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (inBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tileDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tilePath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// Update every tile in the pivot row.  The completed pivot tile is read only;
+// each block owns one destination tile and advances through its local k values
+// in the same order as the scalar algorithm.
+__global__ void floydRowKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                               unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                               const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool pivotInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool rowInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+
+    pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    if (rowInBounds) {
+        rowDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+        rowPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        rowDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        rowPath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = pivotDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = rowDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && rowInBounds && candidate < current) {
+            rowDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            rowPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (rowInBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowPath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// Update every tile in the pivot column.  This is the transpose of the pivot
+// row phase, but uses the original column-major layout directly.
+__global__ void floydColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                  unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                  const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool pivotInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool columnInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+
+    pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    if (columnInBounds) {
+        columnDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+        columnPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        columnDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        columnPath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       pivotDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = columnDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && columnInBounds && candidate < current) {
+            columnDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            columnPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (columnInBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnPath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// All non-pivot tiles are independent once their matching pivot row and column
+// have been completed.  One thread updates one matrix element, retaining its
+// value in a register while consuming the two shared-memory input tiles.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                    unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                    const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile || destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool columnInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool rowInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+    const bool outputInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+
+    columnDist[localIndex] = columnInBounds ? dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    rowDist[localIndex] = rowInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (outputInBounds) {
+        unsigned int current = dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+        unsigned int currentPath = path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+            if (localK < pivotWidth) {
+                const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                               rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+                if (candidate < current) {
+                    current = candidate;
+                    currentPath = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = current;
+        path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = currentPath;
+    }
+}

                               const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
     unsigned int seed = 42;

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {

-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];

+double floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t matrixElements = numNodes * numNodes;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = matrixElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, matrixBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, matrixBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, dist.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, path.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 tileLine(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+    const dim3 tilePlane(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
+                         static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
+    for (size_t pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydRowKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydColumnKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydRemainingKernel<<<tilePlane, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dist.data(), deviceDist, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(path.data(), devicePath, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
 }

+
+    if (inBounds) {
+        tileDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+        tilePath[localIndex] = path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        tileDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        tilePath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = tileDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       tileDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = tileDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && inBounds && candidate < current) {
+            tileDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            tilePath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (inBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tileDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tilePath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// Update every tile in the pivot row.  The completed pivot tile is read only;
+// each block owns one destination tile and advances through its local k values
+// in the same order as the scalar algorithm.
+__global__ void floydRowKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                               unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                               const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool pivotInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool rowInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+
+    pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    if (rowInBounds) {
+        rowDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+        rowPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        rowDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        rowPath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = pivotDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = rowDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && rowInBounds && candidate < current) {
+            rowDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            rowPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (rowInBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowPath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// Update every tile in the pivot column.  This is the transpose of the pivot
+// row phase, but uses the original column-major layout directly.
+__global__ void floydColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                  unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                  const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool pivotInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool columnInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+
+    pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    if (columnInBounds) {
+        columnDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+        columnPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        columnDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        columnPath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       pivotDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = columnDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && columnInBounds && candidate < current) {
+            columnDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            columnPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (columnInBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnPath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// All non-pivot tiles are independent once their matching pivot row and column
+// have been completed.  One thread updates one matrix element, retaining its
+// value in a register while consuming the two shared-memory input tiles.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                    unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                    const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile || destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool columnInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool rowInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+    const bool outputInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+
+    columnDist[localIndex] = columnInBounds ? dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    rowDist[localIndex] = rowInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (outputInBounds) {
+        unsigned int current = dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+        unsigned int currentPath = path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+            if (localK < pivotWidth) {
+                const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                               rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+                if (candidate < current) {
+                    current = candidate;
+                    currentPath = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = current;
+        path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = currentPath;
+    }
+}
+

+double floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t matrixElements = numNodes * numNodes;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = matrixElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, matrixBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, matrixBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, dist.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, path.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 tileLine(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+    const dim3 tilePlane(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
+                         static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
+    for (size_t pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydRowKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydColumnKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydRemainingKernel<<<tilePlane, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dist.data(), deviceDist, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(path.data(), devicePath, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
 }

+__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j,
+                                                  const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
 }
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                          \
+    do {                                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (call);                                                    \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                          \

+// Matrix entries are stored as matrix[column][row].  Keeping threadIdx.x on the
+// row dimension makes every warp's global loads and stores contiguous.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tileDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int tilePath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t i = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t j = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool inBounds = i < numNodes && j < numNodes;
+
+    if (inBounds) {
+        tileDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+        tilePath[localIndex] = path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        tileDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        tilePath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = tileDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       tileDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = tileDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && inBounds && candidate < current) {
+            tileDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            tilePath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (inBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tileDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tilePath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// Update every tile in the pivot row.  The completed pivot tile is read only;
+// each block owns one destination tile and advances through its local k values
+// in the same order as the scalar algorithm.
+__global__ void floydRowKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                               unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                               const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool pivotInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool rowInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+
+    pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    if (rowInBounds) {
+        rowDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+        rowPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        rowDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        rowPath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = pivotDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = rowDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && rowInBounds && candidate < current) {
+            rowDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            rowPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (rowInBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowPath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// Update every tile in the pivot column.  This is the transpose of the pivot
+// row phase, but uses the original column-major layout directly.
+__global__ void floydColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                  unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                  const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool pivotInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool columnInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+
+    pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    if (columnInBounds) {
+        columnDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+        columnPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        columnDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        columnPath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       pivotDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = columnDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && columnInBounds && candidate < current) {
+            columnDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            columnPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (columnInBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnPath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// All non-pivot tiles are independent once their matching pivot row and column
+// have been completed.  One thread updates one matrix element, retaining its
+// value in a register while consuming the two shared-memory input tiles.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                    unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                    const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile || destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool columnInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool rowInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+    const bool outputInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+
+    columnDist[localIndex] = columnInBounds ? dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    rowDist[localIndex] = rowInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (outputInBounds) {
+        unsigned int current = dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+        unsigned int currentPath = path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+            if (localK < pivotWidth) {
+                const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                               rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+                if (candidate < current) {
+                    current = candidate;
+                    currentPath = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = current;
+        path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = currentPath;
+    }
+}

+double floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+
+    const size_t matrixElements = numNodes * numNodes;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = matrixElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, matrixBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, matrixBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, dist.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, path.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 tileLine(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+    const dim3 tilePlane(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
+                         static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
+    for (size_t pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydRowKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydColumnKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydRemainingKernel<<<tilePlane, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dist.data(), deviceDist, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(path.data(), devicePath, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
 }

   250	
   251	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
   252	                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
   253	    unsigned int seed = 42;
   254	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
   255	    
   256	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
   257	        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
   258	    }
   259	    
   260	    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
   261	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   262	        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
   263	    }
   264	}
   265	
   266	void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
   267	    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
   268	        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   269	            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
   270	            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
   271	        }
   272	        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
   273	    }
   274	}
   275	
   276	double floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   277	                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
   278	                     const size_t numNodes) {
   279	    if (numNodes == 0) {
   280	        return 0.0;
   281	    }
   282	
   283	    const size_t matrixElements = numNodes * numNodes;
   284	    const size_t matrixBytes = matrixElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
   285	    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
   286	    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
   287	    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
   288	    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
   289	
   290	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, matrixBytes));
   291	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, matrixBytes));
   292	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, dist.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   293	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, path.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   294	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
   295	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
   296	
   297	    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
   298	    const dim3 block(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
   299	    const dim3 tileLine(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
   300	    const dim3 tilePlane(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
   301	                         static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
   302	
   303	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
   304	    for (size_t pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
   305	        floydPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
   306	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   307	
   308	        floydRowKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
   309	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   310	
   311	        floydColumnKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
   312	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   313	
   314	        floydRemainingKernel<<<tilePlane, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
   315	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   316	    }
   317	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
   318	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
   319	
   320	    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   321	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
   322	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dist.data(), deviceDist, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   323	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(path.data(), devicePath, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   324	
   325	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
   326	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
   327	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
   328	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
   329	    return static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds);
   330	}
   331	
   332	bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
   333	    // Basic sanity checks
   334	    
   335	    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
   336	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   337	        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
   338	            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
   339	            return false;
   340	        }
   341	    }
   342	    
   343	    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
   344	    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
   345	    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
   346	        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
   347	            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
   348	                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
   349	                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
   350	                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
   351	                
   352	                // Check for overflow before addition
   353	                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
   354	                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
   355	                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
   356	                               i, j, k);
   357	                        return false;
   358	                    }
   359	                }
   360	            }

 #include <cstring>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
 
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
-inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
+__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j,
+                                                  const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
 }
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                          \
+    do {                                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (call);                                                    \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                          \
+            fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,                  \
+                    cudaGetErrorString(cudaStatus));                                              \
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                              \
+        }                                                                                         \
+    } while (false)

+// Matrix entries are stored as matrix[column][row].  Keeping threadIdx.x on the
+// row dimension makes every warp's global loads and stores contiguous.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tileDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int tilePath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t i = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t j = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool inBounds = i < numNodes && j < numNodes;
+
+    if (inBounds) {
+        tileDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+        tilePath[localIndex] = path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        tileDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        tilePath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = tileDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       tileDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = tileDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && inBounds && candidate < current) {
+            tileDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            tilePath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (inBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tileDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tilePath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// Update every tile in the pivot row.  The completed pivot tile is read only;
+// each block owns one destination tile and advances through its local k values
+// in the same order as the scalar algorithm.
+__global__ void floydRowKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                               unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                               const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool pivotInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool rowInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+
+    pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    if (rowInBounds) {
+        rowDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+        rowPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        rowDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        rowPath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = pivotDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = rowDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && rowInBounds && candidate < current) {
+            rowDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            rowPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (rowInBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowPath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// Update every tile in the pivot column.  This is the transpose of the pivot
+// row phase, but uses the original column-major layout directly.
+__global__ void floydColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                  unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                  const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool pivotInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool columnInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+
+    pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    if (columnInBounds) {
+        columnDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+        columnPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+    } else {
+        columnDist[localIndex] = INF;
+        columnPath[localIndex] = 0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                       pivotDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+        const unsigned int current = columnDist[localIndex];
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (activeK && columnInBounds && candidate < current) {
+            columnDist[localIndex] = candidate;
+            columnPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (columnInBounds) {
+        dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnDist[localIndex];
+        path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnPath[localIndex];
+    }
+}
+
+// All non-pivot tiles are independent once their matching pivot row and column
+// have been completed.  One thread updates one matrix element, retaining its
+// value in a register while consuming the two shared-memory input tiles.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                    unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                    const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile || destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int localI = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localJ = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+    const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+    const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+    const bool columnInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes;
+    const bool rowInBounds = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+    const bool outputInBounds = sourceI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+
+    columnDist[localIndex] = columnInBounds ? dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    rowDist[localIndex] = rowInBounds ? dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] : INF;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (outputInBounds) {
+        unsigned int current = dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+        unsigned int currentPath = path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+            if (localK < pivotWidth) {
+                const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                               rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+                if (candidate < current) {
+                    current = candidate;
+                    currentPath = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = current;
+        path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = currentPath;
+    }
+}

+                     const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 16;
+constexpr int ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_SIZE;
+
+static_assert(TILE_SIZE % BLOCK_SIZE == 0);
 
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
-inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
+__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j,
+                                                  const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
 }
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                          \
+    do {                                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (call);                                                    \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                          \

+// Matrix entries are stored as matrix[column][row].  A 16x16 thread block owns
+// a 32x32 tile, so each thread handles a 2x2 sub-tile.  This retains fully
+// coalesced accesses while allowing enough blocks to reside on each SM.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tileDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int tilePath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t j = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+            const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t i = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+            if (i < numNodes && j < numNodes) {
+                tileDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+                tilePath[localIndex] = path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)];
+            } else {
+                tileDist[localIndex] = INF;
+                tilePath[localIndex] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        unsigned int candidate[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+        unsigned int current[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+            const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+                const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+                candidate[tileJ][tileI] = tileDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                          tileDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+                current[tileJ][tileI] = tileDist[localIndex];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+            const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t j = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+                const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const size_t i = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+                const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+                if (activeK && i < numNodes && j < numNodes &&
+                    candidate[tileJ][tileI] < current[tileJ][tileI]) {
+                    tileDist[localIndex] = candidate[tileJ][tileI];
+                    tilePath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t j = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+            const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t i = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+            if (i < numNodes && j < numNodes) {
+                dist[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tileDist[localIndex];
+                path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = tilePath[localIndex];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Update every tile in the pivot row.  The completed pivot tile is read only;
+// each block owns one destination tile and advances through its local k values
+// in the same order as the scalar algorithm.
+__global__ void floydRowKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                               unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                               const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+            const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+            pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes
+                                        ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes))
+                                        : INF;
+            if (pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes) {
+                rowDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+                rowPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+            } else {
+                rowDist[localIndex] = INF;
+                rowPath[localIndex] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        unsigned int candidate[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+        unsigned int current[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+            const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+                const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+                candidate[tileJ][tileI] = pivotDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                          rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+                current[tileJ][tileI] = rowDist[localIndex];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+            const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+                const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+                const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+                if (activeK && pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes &&
+                    candidate[tileJ][tileI] < current[tileJ][tileI]) {
+                    rowDist[localIndex] = candidate[tileJ][tileI];
+                    rowPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+            const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+            if (pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes) {
+                dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowDist[localIndex];
+                path[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = rowPath[localIndex];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Update every tile in the pivot column.  This is the transpose of the pivot
+// row phase, but uses the original column-major layout directly.
+__global__ void floydColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                  unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                  const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnPath[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+            const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+            pivotDist[localIndex] = pivotI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes
+                                        ? dist[idx2(pivotI, pivotJ, numNodes)]
+                                        : INF;
+            if (sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes) {
+                columnDist[localIndex] = dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+                columnPath[localIndex] = path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)];
+            } else {
+                columnDist[localIndex] = INF;
+                columnPath[localIndex] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        const bool activeK = localK < pivotWidth;
+        unsigned int candidate[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+        unsigned int current[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+            const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+                const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+                candidate[tileJ][tileI] = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                          pivotDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+                current[tileJ][tileI] = columnDist[localIndex];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+            const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+                const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+                const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+                if (activeK && sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes &&
+                    candidate[tileJ][tileI] < current[tileJ][tileI]) {
+                    columnDist[localIndex] = candidate[tileJ][tileI];
+                    columnPath[localIndex] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {

+                path[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)] = columnPath[localIndex];

+// All non-pivot tiles are independent once their matching pivot row and column
+// have been completed.  Each thread updates a 2x2 output sub-tile from the two
+// shared-memory input tiles, retaining all four values in registers.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                    unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                    const size_t numNodes, const size_t pivotTile) {
+    const size_t sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    const size_t destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile == pivotTile || destinationTile == pivotTile) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowDist[TILE_SIZE * TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const size_t sourceStart = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t destinationStart = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotStart = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t pivotRemaining = numNodes - pivotStart;
+    const int pivotWidth = pivotRemaining < TILE_SIZE ? static_cast<int>(pivotRemaining) : TILE_SIZE;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t pivotJ = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+            const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            const size_t pivotI = pivotStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            const int localIndex = localJ * TILE_SIZE + localI;
+            columnDist[localIndex] = sourceI < numNodes && pivotJ < numNodes
+                                         ? dist[idx2(sourceI, pivotJ, numNodes)]
+                                         : INF;
+            rowDist[localIndex] = pivotI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes
+                                      ? dist[idx2(pivotI, destinationJ, numNodes)]
+                                      : INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    unsigned int current[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int currentPath[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+    bool outputInBounds[ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD][ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+            const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            outputInBounds[tileJ][tileI] = sourceI < numNodes && destinationJ < numNodes;
+            if (outputInBounds[tileJ][tileI]) {
+                current[tileJ][tileI] = dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+                currentPath[tileJ][tileI] = path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)];
+            } else {
+                current[tileJ][tileI] = INF;
+                currentPath[tileJ][tileI] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int localK = 0; localK < TILE_SIZE; ++localK) {
+        if (localK < pivotWidth) {
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+                const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+                    const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+                    const unsigned int candidate = columnDist[localK * TILE_SIZE + localI] +
+                                                   rowDist[localJ * TILE_SIZE + localK];
+                    if (outputInBounds[tileJ][tileI] && candidate < current[tileJ][tileI]) {
+                        current[tileJ][tileI] = candidate;
+                        currentPath[tileJ][tileI] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivotStart + localK);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int tileJ = 0; tileJ < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileJ) {
+        const int localJ = threadIdx.y + tileJ * BLOCK_SIZE;
+        const size_t destinationJ = destinationStart + static_cast<size_t>(localJ);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int tileI = 0; tileI < ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD; ++tileI) {
+            const int localI = threadIdx.x + tileI * BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t sourceI = sourceStart + static_cast<size_t>(localI);
+            if (outputInBounds[tileJ][tileI]) {
+                dist[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = current[tileJ][tileI];
+                path[idx2(sourceI, destinationJ, numNodes)] = currentPath[tileJ][tileI];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+double floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0;
     }
+
+    const size_t matrixElements = numNodes * numNodes;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = matrixElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, matrixBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, matrixBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, dist.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, path.data(), matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 tileLine(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+    const dim3 tilePlane(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
+                         static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
+    for (size_t pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydRowKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydColumnKernel<<<tileLine, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        floydRemainingKernel<<<tilePlane, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dist.data(), deviceDist, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(path.data(), devicePath, matrixBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;

+constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 16;
+constexpr int ELEMENTS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_SIZE;

+__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j,
+                                                  const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
 }
 
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                          \
+    do {                                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (call);                                                    \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                          \
